{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/bench/autotune_fp4/bench_autotune.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Bench autotune FP4 kernels vs sgl-kernel baseline.\n\nFor each shape × each compiled .so: allocate NVFP4-quantized inputs via\nsgl-kernel's fp4_quantize (same layout as autotune_kernel.cu), dlopen the\n.so, call run_gemm, measure time.\n\nOutput: bench/autotune_fp4/out/results.json\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\nimport ctypes\nimport glob\nimport json\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\nfrom sgl_kernel import (\n    cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm as baseline_fp4_gemm,\n    scaled_fp4_quant as fp4_quantize,\n)\n\nHERE = Path(__file__).parent\nOUT_DIR = HERE / \"out\"\n\nSHAPES = [\n    (\"q_proj\",    4096,  4096),\n    (\"o_proj\",    4096,  4096),\n    (\"gate_proj\", 4096, 16384),\n    (\"up_proj\",   4096, 16384),\n    (\"down_proj\", 16384, 4096),\n]\nM_LIST = [8192, 2048]\nWARMUP = 10\nITERS = 50\nREPEATS = 3\n\n\ndef load_lib(path: str):\n    lib = ctypes.CDLL(path)\n    fn = lib.run_gemm\n    # (A, Asf, B, Bsf, alpha_f, D, M, N, K, stream)\n    fn.argtypes = [\n        ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p,\n        ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p,\n        ctypes.c_float,\n        ctypes.c_void_p,\n        ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int,\n        ctypes.c_void_p,\n    ]\n    fn.restype = ctypes.c_int\n    return fn\n\n\ndef time_fn(run):\n    for _ in range(WARMUP):\n        run()\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    best = float(\"inf\")\n    for _ in range(REPEATS):\n        s = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n        e = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n        s.record()\n        for _ in range(ITERS):\n            run()\n        e.record()\n        torch.cuda.synchronize()\n        t = s.elapsed_time(e) / ITERS\n        if t < best:\n            best = t\n    return best\n\n\ndef bench_one_shape(label: str, K: int, N: int, libs: dict):\n    rows = []\n    for M in M_LIST:\n        x_bf16 = torch.randn(M, K, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=\"cuda\")\n        w_bf16 = torch.randn(N, K, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=\"cuda\")\n\n        inp_inv = torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n        w_inv = torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n        xq, xs = fp4_quantize(x_bf16, inp_inv)\n        wq, ws = fp4_quantize(w_bf16, w_inv)\n        alpha = torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n\n        # baseline\n        def run_baseline():\n            baseline_fp4_gemm(xq, wq, xs, ws, alpha, torch.bfloat16)\n        t_baseline = time_fn(run_baseline)\n\n        out = torch.empty((M, N), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=\"cuda\")\n        stream = torch.cuda.current_stream().cuda_stream\n\n        lib_times = {}\n        for tag, fn in libs.items():\n            def run_lib(fn=fn, M=M, N=N, K=K):\n                rc = fn(\n                    xq.data_ptr(), xs.data_ptr(),\n                    wq.data_ptr(), ws.data_ptr(),\n                    1.0,\n                    out.data_ptr(),\n                    M, N, K,\n                    stream,\n                )\n                if rc != 0:\n                    raise RuntimeError(f\"{tag}: run_gemm rc={rc}\")\n            try:\n                t = time_fn(run_lib)\n                lib_times[tag] = t\n            except Exception as ex:\n                print(f\"  [FAIL] {label} M={M} tag={tag}: {ex}\")\n                lib_times[tag] = None\n\n        flops = 2 * M * N * K\n        row = {\n            \"shape\": label, \"M\": M, \"N\": N, \"K\": K,\n            \"baseline_us\": t_baseline * 1e3,\n            \"baseline_tflops\": flops / (t_baseline * 1e-3) / 1e12,\n            \"libs\": {\n                tag: ({\n                    \"us\": t * 1e3,\n                    \"tflops\": flops / (t * 1e-3) / 1e12,\n                    \"speedup_vs_baseline\": t_baseline / t,\n                } if t is not None else None)\n                for tag, t in lib_times.items()\n            },\n        }\n        rows.append(row)\n        print(f\"{label:>10} M={M:>5}  baseline {t_baseline*1e3:7.1f}us \"\n              f\"({flops/(t_baseline*1e-3)/1e12:6.1f} TF)\")\n        best_tag = None\n        best_t = t_baseline\n        for tag, t in lib_times.items():\n            if t is not None and t < best_t:\n                best_t = t\n                best_tag = tag\n        if best_tag:\n            print(f\"            best lib: {best_tag} \"\n                  f\"{best_t*1e3:.1f}us ({flops/(best_t*1e-3)/1e12:.1f} TF) \"\n                  f\"speedup {t_baseline/best_t:.3f}x\")\n    return rows\n\n\ndef main():\n    libs = {}\n    for so in sorted(glob.glob(str(OUT_DIR / \"lib_*.so\"))):\n        tag = Path(so).stem.replace(\"lib_\", \"\")\n        try:\n            libs[tag] = load_lib(so)\n        except Exception as ex:\n            print(f\"load fail {tag}: {ex}\")\n    print(f\"loaded {len(libs)} libs\")\n\n    all_rows = []\n    for label, K, N in SHAPES:\n        print(f\"\\n=== {label} K={K} N={N} ===\")\n        all_rows.extend(bench_one_shape(label, K, N, libs))\n\n    with open(OUT_DIR / \"results.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(all_rows, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nsaved: {OUT_DIR / 'results.json'}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

as fp4_quantize\n\nSHAPES = [\n    (\"q_proj\",    4096,  4096),\n    (\"o_proj\",    4096,  4096),\n    (\"gate_proj\", 4096, 16384),\n    (\"up_proj\",   4096, 16384),\n    (\"down_proj\", 16384, 4096),\n]\nM_LIST = [8192, 2048]\nWARMUP = 10\nITERS = 50\nREPEATS = 3\n\n\ndef time_fn(run):\n    for _ in range(WARMUP):\n        run()\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    best = float(\"inf\")\n    for _ in range(REPEATS):\n        s = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n        e = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n        s.record()\n        for _ in range(ITERS):\n            run()\n        e.record()\n        torch.cuda.synchronize()\n        t = s.elapsed_time(e) / ITERS\n        if t < best:\n            best = t\n    return best\n\n\ndef main():\n    so = sys.argv[1]\n    tag = Path(so).stem.replace(\"lib_\", \"\")\n\n    lib = ctypes.CDLL(so)\n    fn = lib.run_gemm\n    fn.argtypes = [\n        ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p,\n        ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p,\n        ctypes.c_float,\n        ctypes.c_void_p,\n        ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_int,\n        ctypes.c_void_p,\n    ]\n    fn.restype = ctypes.c_int\n\n    rows = []\n    for label, K, N in SHAPES:\n        for M in M_LIST:\n            x_bf16 = torch.randn(M, K, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=\"cuda\")\n            w_bf16 = torch.randn(N, K, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=\"cuda\")\n            inp_inv = torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n            w_inv = torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n            xq, xs = fp4_quantize(x_bf16, inp_inv)\n            wq, ws = fp4_quantize(w_bf16, w_inv)\n            out = torch.empty((M, N), dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=\"cuda\")\n            stream = torch.cuda.current_stream().cuda_stream\n\n            def run(M=M, N=N, K=K):\n                rc = fn(\n                    xq.data_ptr(), xs.data_ptr(),\n                    wq.data_ptr(), ws.data_ptr(),\n                    1.0,\n                    out.data_ptr(),\n                    M, N, K,\n                    stream,\n                )\n                if rc != 0:\n                    raise RuntimeError(f\"rc={rc}\")\n\n            try:\n                # small warmup first; if it crashes it crashes\n                run()\n                torch.cuda.synchronize()\n                t = time_fn(run)\n                flops = 2 * M * N * K\n                rows.append({\n                    \"tag\": tag, \"shape\": label, \"M\": M, \"N\": N, \"K\": K,\n                    \"us\": t * 1e3,\n                    \"tflops\": flops / (t * 1e-3) / 1e12,\n                })\n            except Exception as ex:\n                rows.append({\n                    \"tag\": tag, \"shape\": label, \"M\": M, \"N\": N, \"K\": K,\n                    \"error\": str(ex),\n                })\n\n    print(json.dumps(rows))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

glob\nimport json\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\nfrom sgl_kernel import (\n    cutlass_scaled_fp4_mm as baseline_fp4_gemm,\n    scaled_fp4_quant as fp4_quantize,\n)\n\nHERE = Path(__file__).parent\nOUT = HERE / \"out\"\n\nSHAPES = [\n    (\"q_proj\",    4096,  4096),\n    (\"o_proj\",    4096,  4096),\n    (\"gate_proj\", 4096, 16384),\n    (\"up_proj\",   4096, 16384),\n    (\"down_proj\", 16384, 4096),\n]\nM_LIST = [8192, 2048]\nWARMUP = 10\nITERS = 50\nREPEATS = 3\n\n\ndef _time(run):\n    for _ in range(WARMUP):\n        run()\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    best = float(\"inf\")\n    for _ in range(REPEATS):\n        s = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n        e = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)\n        s.record()\n        for _ in range(ITERS):\n            run()\n        e.record()\n        torch.cuda.synchronize()\n        t = s.elapsed_time(e) / ITERS\n        if t < best:\n            best = t\n    return best\n\n\ndef run_baseline():\n    rows = []\n    for label, K, N in SHAPES:\n        for M in M_LIST:\n            x_bf16 = torch.randn(M, K, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=\"cuda\")\n            w_bf16 = torch.randn(N, K, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=\"cuda\")\n            inp_inv = torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n            w_inv = torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n            xq, xs = fp4_quantize(x_bf16, inp_inv)\n            wq, ws = fp4_quantize(w_bf16, w_inv)\n            alpha = torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.float32, device=\"cuda\")\n            def run():\n                baseline_fp4_gemm(xq, wq, xs, ws, alpha, torch.bfloat16)\n            t = _time(run)\n            rows.append({\"shape\": label, \"M\": M, \"N\": N, \"K\": K,\n                         \"us\": t * 1e3,\n                         \"tflops\": 2 * M * N * K / (t * 1e-3) / 1e12})\n    return rows\n\n\ndef run_worker(so):\n    tag = Path(so).stem.replace(\"lib_\", \"\")\n    print(f\"[worker] {tag}\", flush=True)\n    res = subprocess.run(\n        [sys.executable, str(HERE / \"bench_worker.py\"), so],\n        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=600,\n    )\n    if res.returncode != 0:\n        # Process aborted (e.g., CUDA illegal memory). Record a shape-independent fail.\n        print(f\"  ABORT: {res.stderr.splitlines()[-1] if res.stderr else 'no stderr'}\")\n        return [{\"tag\": tag, \"aborted\": True, \"stderr_tail\": res.stderr[-2000:]}]\n    try:\n        return json.loads(res.stdout)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        print(f\"  JSON decode fail: {ex}\")\n        print(res.stdout[:500])\n        return [{\"tag\": tag, \"aborted\": True, \"stderr_tail\": res.stderr[-2000:]}]\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=== baseline (sgl-kernel CUTLASS) ===\", flush=True)\n    baseline_rows = run_baseline()\n    for r in baseline_rows:\n        print(f\"{r['shape']:>10} M={r['M']:>5}  {r['us']:7.1f}us  {r['tflops']:6.1f} TF\")\n\n    all_worker_rows = []\n    for so in sorted(glob.glob(str(OUT / \"lib_*.so\"))):\n        all_worker_rows.extend(run_worker(so))\n\n    results = {\n        \"baseline\": baseline_rows,\n        \"workers\": all_worker_rows,\n    }\n    with open(OUT / \"results.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nsaved: {OUT / 'results.json'}\")\n\n    # Quick summary per shape×M\n    print(\"\\n=== Summary (best tag per shape×M vs baseline) ===\")\n    by_key = {}\n    for r in all_worker_rows:\n        if \"us\" not in r:\n            continue\n        key = (r[\"shape\"], r[\"M\"])\n        by_key.setdefault(key, []).append(r)\n    for r in baseline_rows:\n        key = (r[\"shape\"], r[\"M\"])\n        bests = sorted(by_key.get(key, []), key=lambda x: x[\"us\"])\n        if not bests:\n            print(f\"{r['shape']:>10} M={r['M']:>5}  baseline {r['us']:7.1f}us  (no workers)\")\n            continue\n        best = bests[0]\n        sp = r[\"us\"] / best[\"us\"]\n        marker = \"✓\" if sp > 1.02 else (\"=\" if sp > 0.98 else \"✗\")\n        print(f\"{r['shape']:>10} M={r['M']:>5}  base {r['us']:7.1f}us  \"\n              f\"best[{best['tag']:>16}] {best['us']:7.1f}us  speedup {sp:.3f}x {marker}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

> AGENT

找到根因：模板 `alpha_ptr` 指向**host** 内存（`static float s_alpha`），但 CUTLASS epilogue 在设备端 dereference → illegal memory。sgl-kernel 传的是 **device** 指针。

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/bench/autotune_fp4/autotune_kernel.cu",
  "old_string": "  static float s_alpha;\n  s_alpha = alpha_f;\n\n  using GemmElementA = typename Gemm::ElementA;",
  "new_string": "  static float* d_alpha = nullptr;\n  if (!d_alpha) {\n    cudaMalloc(&d_alpha, sizeof(float));\n  }\n  cudaMemcpyAsync(d_alpha, &alpha_f, sizeof(float), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream);\n\n  using GemmElementA = typename Gemm::ElementA;"
}
```

Fix 已应用。你重跑前请先 rebuild：

```bash
cd /user_4813494d/openbmb/bench/autotune_fp4 && bash build.sh && python3 bench_autotune.py
```

**根因**：CUTLASS epilogue 的 `alpha_ptr` 必须指向 device 内存——原来写的 `static float s_alpha` 是 host stack，GPU 读它直接 illegal address。改成 `cudaMalloc + cudaMemcpyAsync`，指针常驻 device。

> AGENT

**粗搜结论：主战场几乎无收益**

| M | 场景 | 平均 speedup | 评估 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 8192 | prefill 主力 | **1.008–1.030×** | 持平/微涨，端到端贡献 <0.5% |
| 2048 | chunked last chunk | 1.04–1.34× | o_proj 1.34× 疑似 baseline 噪声（q_proj 同 shape 1.05×） |

**关键观察**：
- 粗搜最常胜出的是 `_s0`（Auto schedule）——CUTLASS `KernelScheduleAuto` **已在选最优 schedule**，手动强制 Cooperative/Pingpong 多数情况反而持平或略逊
- prefill 主力 M=8192 四个大 GEMM（gate/up/down 占比最高）全部 ≤1.03×，和 investigation.md 预判 "560→750-1000 TF" 的 1.3-1.8× 差距巨大——**sm_120 NVFP4 on CUTLASS 已接近局部最优，不是"调优大洼地"**
- 真实上限 1467 TFLOPS 和实测 ~550 之间的 gap 是**ISA/smem/scale-load 架构级开销**，不是 scheduler 选错

**按 CLAUDE.md 最高指令评估**：预期端到端 10-15% 没兑现，实际 <1%。autotune 这条路**结束**。

**下一条**：§6.3 epilogue fusion（SwiGLU 融入 gate+up，省中间 32 MB activation 的 write+read）——这个跳出 GEMM kernel 内部的博弈，收益来源独立。

要我转去做 epilogue fusion 的可行性调研（先看 sgl-kernel 当前 SwiGLU 路径 + CUTLASS epilogue 能不能塞进去），还是想先细搜 stages（收益预期更小）？

完美！现在我有了足够的信息。让我总结报告：

---

## 调研报告：SwiGLU Epilogue Fusion 可行性评估

### Q1. SALA MLP 当前路径

**答案**：MiniCPM-SALA 已使用 **fused gate+up** GEMM，但 SwiGLU 仍为独立 kernel。

**具体代码**：
- `/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/models/minicpm.py:191-216`
  - `MiniCPMMLP.gate_up_proj = MergedColumnParallelLinear(hidden_size, [intermediate_size]*2, ...)`
  - 行 213：`gate_up, _ = self.gate_up_proj(x)` ← 一个 GEMM 输出 2×N（拼接状态）
  - 行 214：`x = self.act_fn(gate_up)` ← SwiGLU 为独立 pointwise kernel

- `/user_4813494d/openbmb/probe-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/activation.py:62-77`
  - `SiluAndMul.forward_cuda()` 调用 `silu_and_mul(x, out)` ← **分离 kernel**，不在 GEMM epilogue 中

**量化路径**：当前使用 **Marlin W4A16 Fallback**
- `/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/marlin_utils_fp4.py:1-50`
  - 行 49：`should_use_fp4_marlin_fallback()` 在非 Blackwell GPU 上使用 Marlin（group_size=16）
  - Blackwell 上会走 CUTLASS NVFP4 BlockScaled 路径

**结论**：当前架构是 **gate_up 两路已融一个 GEMM** → **SwiGLU 独立 kernel**。改进空间在于将 SwiGLU 融入 GEMM epilogue。

---

### Q2. CUTLASS FP4 GEMM 的 Epilogue 扩展点

**答案**：当前 epilogue 仅支持 `LinearCombination`（alpha×AB），不支持 gated/SiLU 操作。

**具体代码**：
- `/user_4813494d/fouroversix/src/fouroversix/csrc/fp4_gemm_sm120.cu:72-80`
  ```cpp
  using CollectiveEpilogue = typename cutlass::epilogue::collective::CollectiveBuilder<
      ArchTag, OperatorClass,
      MmaTileShape, ClusterShape,
      cutlass::epilogue::collective::EpilogueTileAuto,
      ElementAccumulator, ElementAccumulator,
      ElementC, LayoutCTag, AlignmentC,
      ElementD, LayoutDTag, AlignmentD,
      cutlass::epilogue::collective::EpilogueScheduleAuto // ← 自动选择
  >::CollectiveOp;
  ```

- `/user_4813494d/backups/cu12-baseline-20260420/packages/sglang-minicpm/sgl-kernel/csrc/gemm/nvfp4_scaled_mm_kernels.cu:166-181`
  ```cpp
  using CollectiveEpilogue = typename cutlass::epilogue::collective::CollectiveBuilder<
      ArchTag, OperatorClass, MmaTileShape, ClusterShape,
      EpilogueTile,
      ElementAccumulator, ElementAccumulator,
      void, LayoutCTag, AlignmentC,
      ElementD, LayoutDTag, AlignmentD,
      EpilogueSchedule,
      cutlass::epilogue::fusion::LinearCombination<ElementD, float, void, float>
  >::CollectiveOp; // ← 仅 alpha*AB + beta*C
  ```

**EpilogueScheduleTag**：`TmaWarpSpecialized1Sm` / `TmaWarpSpecialized2Sm` / `EpilogueScheduleAuto`

**扩展需求**：要改 epilogue，需改动这两处代码，**必须重新编译 kernel lib**。

---

### Q3. CUTLASS 内置 SwiGLU/Gated Epilogue

**答案**：**有** `LinearCombinationSilu`，但 **不支持 dual output（gate 和 up）**。

**具体代码**：
- `/user_4813494d/.cache/uv/archive-v0/2_lAAShcaQdoohLC7E9jM/flashinfer/data/cutlass/include/cutlass/epilogue/thread/linear_combination_silu.h:49-64`
  ```cpp
  /// D = silu(alpha * accumulator + beta * source + uniform)
  template <typename ElementOutput_, int Count, ...>
  using LinearCombinationSilu = LinearCombinationGeneric<SiLu, ElementOutput_, Count, ...>;
  ```
  ← **单路输出**，不是 dual-gated

- 现有 `linear_combination_*.h` 文件扫描结果：`linear_combination_silu.h` 存在，但无 `linear_combination_gated.h` / `linear_combination_swiglu.h` / `linear_combination_dual*.h`

**关键约束**（未找到显式文档）：
- `/user_4813494d/.cache/uv/archive-v0/2_lAAShcaQdoohLC7E9jM/flashinfer/data/cutlass/include/cutlass/epilogue/collective/sm100_epilogue_tma_warpspecialized.hpp:84+`
  - `class CollectiveEpilogue` 的设计是 **per-tile 单路输出**，每个 tile 处理一个数据块并存储一次
  - **Architecture constraint**：CUTLASS epilogue 在 tile 层次处理，不支持同一 tile 内的 gate 和 up partial accumulators 分离
  
**第三方实现**：未在 TRT-LLM 或 sgl-kernel 中找到 dual-GEMM + SwiGLU fused kernel。

---

### Q4. 工作量评估

**答案**：**大工作**（500+ 行），且有 show-stopper。

| 方面 | 评估 |
|------|------|
| **量级** | 500+ 行（新 epilogue 模板 + 集成代码） |
| **Show-stopper** | **有**：CUTLASS SM120 block-scaled op 的 epilogue 架构设计为 per-tile 单路输出。要支持 dual output（gate/up 共享一个 accumulator 再分裂），需修改 epilogue collective 的核心框架，非平凡 |
| **Rebuild sgl-kernel** | **必须** |
| **可行替代方案** | 改 Python 层：将 gate_up split 提前到 GEMM 前，输出两个 separate tensors；然后两个 kernel 分别做 down_proj（利用 FP4 GEMM 的 alpha 参数做 SiLU）← **可行但不优雅** |

**具体障碍**：
- 行号 `/user_4813494d/.cache/uv/archive-v0/2_lAAShcaQdoohLC7E9jM/flashinfer/data/cutlass/include/cutlass/epilogue/collective/sm100_epilogue_tma_warpspecialized.hpp:84+` 的 `CollectiveEpilogue` 类的设计假设单一数据流路径
- SM120 block-scaled 与 dual output 的兼容性未知，需实验验证

---

## 500字总结

**不建议做 SwiGLU epilogue fusion**。虽然 CUTLASS 有 `LinearCombinationSilu`，但这是单路的，无法在 epilogue 内同时产生 gate 和 up 两个输出。CUTLASS per-tile epilogue 架构决定了每个数据块只能有一路数据流出，改成 dual output 需动 epilogue collective 框架代码（500+ 行），且 SM120 block-scaled 下兼容性未知（show-stopper）。

**建议替代方案**：保持 gate_up 合成一个 GEMM（已做），但在 SiluAndMul kernel 层优化。或者，利用 fouroversix/CUTLASS 的 alpha 参数：在 GEMM epilogue 中 apply SiLU-scaled alpha（需修改 `LinearCombination` 的 compute phase，~100 行改动，但有兼容风险）。

> AGENT

**SwiGLU fusion 调研结论：天花板太低，不值得做**

**技术路线图**（四条，按从易到难）：

| 路线 | 可行性 | 省多少 DRAM | 工程量 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 仅 SiLU 融入 gate_up epilogue（`LinCombEltAct<SiLu>`） | CUTLASS 3.x 原生支持 | **0**（mul kernel 还得读 2 份） | 小 |
| EVT + column-conditional SiLU（只 silu gate half） | 可行 | 0（同上） | 中 |
| **Weight interleave**（gate/up 按 N 维交错，单 GEMM tile 内出 gate+up pair） | 可行但需重排 weight + 改量化 | ~768 MB/layer/chunk | 中等（~400 行） |
| **sm_120 FP4 DualGemm kernel**（移植 CUTLASS 2.x `45_dual_gemm` 3000 行到 3.x block-scaled） | 风险高 | ~768 MB/layer/chunk | **数周** |

**收益上限粗算**（最优情形）：
- silu_and_mul kernel ~480 us/layer × 32 layer × 12 chunk（100K prefill）≈ **180 ms**
- 占 prefill ~0.2%，占端到端 <0.1%

**这和 b12x 一样，理论可行但量级不够**。

**真正问题**：我们在盲猜热点。连续两次（autotune 1%，fusion 0.2%）都没打到 1.5× 级别的胜场，说明猜的 hotspot 都不是 hotspot。

**建议转入 profile-driven**：

1. `nsys profile` 跑一次 100K-token prefill + 1 round decode，看 top-20 kernel 占比
2. 对占比 >5% 的 kernel 单独打光
3. 据此定下一个工作

这一步是投资回报最高的动作——把盲攻击换成定向打击。要我直接起 profile？

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
# =============================================================
# mini_bench.sh — 快速速度验证（全量 bench 的 ~25%）
#
# 用法:
#   bash bench/mini_bench.sh [API_BASE] [N_S1] [N_S8] [N_SMAX]
#
# 默认值:
#   API_BASE = http://127.0.0.1:30000
#   N_S1=3  N_S8=8  N_SMAX=16
#
# S8=8 恰好一个满批，能体现并发压力；Smax=16 覆盖长尾。
# 数据集嵌套关系: S1(8) ⊆ S8(24) ⊆ Smax(64)，每档样本唯一。
# 本脚本取各档前 N 条，保留与全量相同的长尾特性。
# =============================================================

API_BASE="${1:-http://127.0.0.1:30000}"
N_S1="${2:-8}"
N_S8="${3:-24}"
N_SMAX="${4:-64}"

BENCH_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
DATA_DIR="${BENCH_DIR}/data"
TOOLKIT_BENCH="${BENCH_DIR}/../toolkit/bench_serving.sh"

echo "=========================================================="
echo "  mini_bench  S1=${N_S1}  S8=${N_S8}  Smax=${N_SMAX}"
echo "  API: ${API_BASE}"
echo "=========================================================="

# 分层抽样到 /tmp（按 prompt_tokens 分布均匀取样，避免前段全是短请求）
S1_MINI="/tmp/mini_s1.jsonl"
S8_MINI="/tmp/mini_s8.jsonl"
SMAX_MINI="/tmp/mini_smax.jsonl"

python3 - <<PY
import json, random

SEED = 42

def stratified_sample(src, dst, n):
    if n <= 0:
        open(dst, 'w').close()  # empty file
        return
    rng = random.Random(SEED)
    items = [json.loads(l) for l in open(src)]
    items.sort(key=lambda x: x['prompt_tokens'])
    step = len(items) / n
    sampled = [rng.choice(items[int(i*step):int((i+1)*step) or len(items)]) for i in range(n)]
--- toolkit eval dataset
cnn_dailymail_calib.jsonl
perf_public_set.jsonl
ultrachat_long_calib.jsonl

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - CUTLASS 3.x CollectiveBuilder with KernelScheduleAuto/Pingpong/Cooperative schedules
   - NVFP4 block-scaled MMA on sm_120 (PTX mma.sync.aligned.kind::mxf4nvf4.block_scale.scale_vec::4X.m16n8k64)
   - StageCountAutoCarveout vs explicit StageCount<N>
   - Epilogue alpha_ptr must be device pointer (not host)
   - TMA descriptor init error 700 = CUDA_ERROR_INVALID_VALUE (illegal memory access from invalid alpha pointer)
   - CUTLASS Epilogue Visitor Tree (EVT) / FusionCallbacks for fusion
   - LinCombEltAct<SiLu> as element-wise activation
   - MiniCPM-SALA: gate_up_proj as MergedColumnParallelLinear (2 GEMMs fused to one)
   - SiluAndMul as separate pointwise kernel
   - CUTLASS 2.x 45_dual_gemm example (Sm80, 3085 lines, non-TMA, non-block-scaled)
   - Process isolation via subprocess.run() for CUDA crash recovery
   - SALA MLP DRAM traffic calculation (gate_up_proj write 512MB, silu_and_mul 768MB, down_proj read 256MB per chunk)

3. Files and Code Sections:
   
   - **/user_4813494d/openbmb/bench/autotune_fp4/autotune_kernel.cu** (186→202 lines)
     - Template CUTLASS sm120 NVFP4 GEMM kernel with configurable tile/cluster/schedule/stages
     - Key changes:
     ```cpp
     #if SCHEDULE == 1
     using KernelScheduleTag   = cutlass::gemm::KernelTmaWarpSpecializedCooperative;
     using EpilogueScheduleTag = cutlass::epilogue::TmaWarpSpecializedCooperative;
     #elif SCHEDULE == 2
     using KernelScheduleTag   = cutlass::gemm::KernelTmaWarpSpecializedPingpong;
     using EpilogueScheduleTag = cutlass::epilogue::TmaWarpSpecialized;
     #else
     using KernelScheduleTag   = cutlass::gemm::collective::KernelScheduleAuto;
     using EpilogueScheduleTag = cutlass::epilogue::collective::EpilogueScheduleAuto;
     #endif

     #endif

     - **Critical alpha_ptr fix** (this was the bug causing all aborts):
     ```cpp
     // WAS: static float s_alpha; s_alpha = alpha_f; args.epilogue.thread.alpha_ptr = &s_alpha;

     static float* d_alpha = nullptr;

     - User feedback: "全部abort 自己解决之后 全部你子集运行来autotune" — demanded both fix AND subprocess isolation
   
   - **Error: After subprocess split, ALL 15 workers still ABORT**
     - user_4813494d cause: Template kernel had `static float s_alpha; args.epilogue.thread.alpha_ptr = &s_alpha;` — CUTLASS epilogue dereferences alpha_ptr on device, host stack pointer → illegal memory access at TMA init
     - Fix: Allocate device memory via `cudaMalloc`, `cudaMemcpyAsync` the scalar, point alpha_ptr there
     - After fix: all 15 configs ran successfully; M=8192 showed 1.008-1.030× gains (negligible)

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
# =============================================================
# mini_bench.sh — 快速速度验证（全量 bench 的 ~25%）
#
# 用法:
#   bash bench/mini_bench.sh [API_BASE] [N_S1] [N_S8] [N_SMAX]
#
# 默认值:
#   API_BASE = http://127.0.0.1:30000
#   N_S1=3  N_S8=8  N_SMAX=16
#
# S8=8 恰好一个满批，能体现并发压力；Smax=16 覆盖长尾。
# 数据集嵌套关系: S1(8) ⊆ S8(24) ⊆ Smax(64)，每档样本唯一。
# 本脚本取各档前 N 条，保留与全量相同的长尾特性。
# =============================================================

API_BASE="${1:-http://127.0.0.1:30000}"
N_S1="${2:-8}"
N_S8="${3:-24}"
N_SMAX="${4:-64}"

BENCH_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
DATA_DIR="${BENCH_DIR}/data"
TOOLKIT_BENCH="${BENCH_DIR}/../toolkit/bench_serving.sh"

echo "=========================================================="
echo "  mini_bench  S1=${N_S1}  S8=${N_S8}  Smax=${N_SMAX}"
echo "  API: ${API_BASE}"
echo "=========================================================="

# 分层抽样到 /tmp（按 prompt_tokens 分布均匀取样，避免前段全是短请求）
S1_MINI="/tmp/mini_s1.jsonl"
S8_MINI="/tmp/mini_s8.jsonl"
SMAX_MINI="/tmp/mini_smax.jsonl"

python3 - <<PY
import json, random

SEED = 42

def stratified_sample(src, dst, n):
    if n <= 0:
        open(dst, 'w').close()  # empty file
        return
    rng = random.Random(SEED)
    items = [json.loads(l) for l in open(src)]
    items.sort(key=lambda x: x['prompt_tokens'])
    step = len(items) / n
    sampled = [rng.choice(items[int(i*step):int((i+1)*step) or len(items)]) for i in range(n)]
    with open(dst, 'w') as f:
        for it in sampled:
            f.write(json.dumps(it, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n')
    pts = [it['prompt_tokens'] for it in sampled]
    print(f"  {dst}: n={n}, prompt avg={sum(pts)//n}, min={min(pts)}, max={max(pts)}")

stratified_sample("${DATA_DIR}/speed_bench_c1.jsonl",         "${S1_MINI}",   ${N_S1})
stratified_sample("${DATA_DIR}/speed_bench_c8.jsonl",         "${S8_MINI}",   ${N_S8})
stratified_sample("${DATA_DIR}/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl", "${SMAX_MINI}", ${N_SMAX})
PY

echo "  抽样完成: S1=${N_S1}条  S8=${N_S8}条  Smax=${N_SMAX}条"
echo ""

SPEED_DATA_S1="${S1_MINI}" \
SPEED_DATA_S8="${S8_MINI}" \
SPEED_DATA_SMAX="${SMAX_MINI}" \
bash "${TOOLKIT_BENCH}" "${API_BASE}"
```

#!/bin/bash

# Wait for server
echo "Waiting for server..."

    curl -s http://127.0.0.1:30000/health > /dev/null 2>&1 && break

done

echo "Sending warmup + profile trigger request..."

echo ""
echo "Check server logs for 'FP8 PROFILE:' lines"
echo "Chrome trace: /tmp/fp8_hybrid_profile.json"

#!/bin/bash
set -euo pipefail
# Kill sglang server safely — 只杀 launch_server 进程及其子进程，不碰 jupyter
# 用法: bash bench/kill_sglang.sh

# 找到 sglang.launch_server 的主进程（排除 grep 自身和 jupyter）
# `ps aux` 在某些环境里会截断命令行，导致 grep 匹配失败；改用 args 全长输出。
find_sglang_pids() {
    ps -eo pid=,args= \
        | grep '[s]glang.launch_server' \
        | grep -v jupyter \
        | awk '{print $1}' \
        || true
}

PIDS=$(find_sglang_pids)

if [ -z "$PIDS" ]; then
    echo "No sglang launch_server running"
    exit 0
fi

for PID in $PIDS; do
    echo "Killing sglang PID=$PID and its children"
    # 先杀子进程树，再杀主进程
    pkill -P "$PID" 2>/dev/null || true
    kill "$PID" 2>/dev/null || true
done

# 等待退出
for i in $(seq 1 15); do
    REMAINING=$(find_sglang_pids)
    if [ -z "$REMAINING" ]; then
        echo "Server stopped"
        exit 0
    fi
    sleep 1
done

# 强制杀
REMAINING=$(find_sglang_pids)
for PID in $REMAINING; do
    echo "Force killing PID=$PID"
    kill -9 "$PID" 2>/dev/null || true
done
sleep 1
echo "Done"
```

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

cd /user_4813494d/openbmb

# EAGLE-3 speculative decoding server
# TTT7 FP4_QAT + RoPE checkpoint, hybrid Marlin decode
#
# Config variants (comment/uncomment):
#   spec_steps=1, topk=2, dtn=3 — 最低 draft 开销, break-even accept_len=1.06
#   spec_steps=3, topk=2, dtn=6 — 更多 draft, break-even accept_len=1.36
#
# 分析: spec_steps=1 时 draft 只需 ~0.5ms, cycle=5.5ms
#        spec_steps=3 时 draft 需 ~1.5ms, cycle=7.0ms
#        accept_len=1.50 → 1步: 1.41x Medusa; 3步: 1.10x Medusa

SPEC_STEPS="${EAGLE_SPEC_STEPS:-2}"
TOPK="${EAGLE_TOPK:-1}"
# dtn = 1 + topk * spec_steps (tree nodes)
DTN=$((1 + TOPK * SPEC_STEPS))
TARGET_MODEL="${EAGLE_TARGET_MODEL:-/user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4}"
DRAFT_MODEL="${EAGLE_DRAFT_MODEL:-/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/sglang_model_v2}"
EAGLE_FORCE_NO_ACCEPT="${EAGLE_FORCE_NO_ACCEPT:-0}" \

```
@app.api_route("/flush_cache", methods=["GET", "POST"])
async def flush_cache():
    """Flush the radix cache."""
    ret = await _global_state.tokenizer_manager.flush_cache()
    return Response(
        content="Cache flushed.\nPlease check backend logs for more details. "
        "(When there are running or waiting requests, the operation will not be performed.)\n",
        status_code=200 if ret.success else HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
    )


@app.api_route("/clear_hicache_storage_backend", methods=["GET", "POST"])
async def clear_hicache_storage_backend():
    """Clear the hierarchical cache storage backend."""
    ret = await _global_state.tokenizer_manager.clear_hicache_storage()
    return Response(
        content="Hierarchical cache storage backend cleared.\n",
        status_code=200 if ret.success else HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
    )


@app.api_route("/start_profile", methods=["GET", "POST"])
async def start_profile_async(obj: Optional[ProfileReqInput] = None):
    """Start profiling."""
    if obj is None:
        obj = ProfileReqInput()

    await _global_state.tokenizer_manager.start_profile(
        output_dir=obj.output_dir,
        start_step=obj.start_step,
        num_steps=obj.num_steps,
        activities=obj.activities,
        with_stack=obj.with_stack,
        record_shapes=obj.record_shapes,
        profile_by_stage=obj.profile_by_stage,
        merge_profiles=obj.merge_profiles,
        profile_prefix=obj.profile_prefix,
        profile_stages=obj.profile_stages,
    )
    return Response(
        content="Start profiling.\n",
        status_code=200,
    )


@app.api_route("/stop_profile", methods=["GET", "POST"])
async def stop_profile_async():
    """Stop profiling."""
    await _global_state.tokenizer_manager.stop_profile()
    return Response(
        content="Stop profiling. This will take some time.\n",
        status_code=200,
    )


@app.api_route("/freeze_gc", methods=["GET", "POST"])
async def freeze_gc_async():
    """
    See engine.freeze_gc for more details.
    """
    await _global_state.tokenizer_manager.freeze_gc()
```

> AGENT

Profile 结果出来了。GPU 总 9.13s（130K + 短 decode 三个请求），kernel 归类：

| 类别 | ms | % | calls |
|---|---|---|---|
| **CUTLASS NVFP4 GEMM** | 3278 | **35.9** | 2640 |
| **InfLLM-v2 stage2** (BatchPrefill dim=16) | 1199 | **13.1** | 136 |
| **Std Attention dim=128** (prefill) | 999 | 11.0 | 15 |
| **Fill/Copy/Add** 散小算子 | 1004 | 11.0 | 33567 |
| **InfLLM-v2 stage1** (flash_fwd_splitkv) | 640 | 7.0 | 136 |
| **InfLLM-v2 support** (block_table/cumsum/...) | 448 | 4.9 | 816 |
| RMSNorm | 320 | 3.5 | 2397 |
| SiLU-and-mul | 312 | 3.4 | 561 |
| NVFP4 quantize (input) | 239 | 2.6 | 2640 |
| GLA chunk_fwd | 210 | 2.3 | 768 |

**归纳**：
- **GEMM 35.9%**：已证明 hardware ceiling（CUTLASS=cuBLAS=cuDNN=sgl-kernel 全部 550 TFLOPS）→ 死路
- **InfLLM-v2 总计 25%**（stage1 7% + stage2 13% + support 5%）→ **最大未调查 target**，stage2 单次 8.8ms × 136 次、stage1 单次 4.7ms，autotune/kernel 内部优化完全空白
- **小算子 11%**（33k 个调用，prefill 不走 cuda graph）→ python/torch glue 开销，fuse 或减少 launch 有收益
- silu/RMSNorm/quantize 合计 9.5% → fuse 进 GEMM 是 3.6 方向的子题，天花板在那

**建议下一步**：深挖 **InfLLM-v2 stage 2 BatchPrefill dim=16 kernel**。它占 13%、156 calls、单次 8.8ms，从未做过 kernel 级 profile。优化 30% → 4% e2e。要不要先打一发 ncu 单点对 stage2 做 kernel-level metric？

384	inline uint32_t FA2DetermineCtaTileQ(int64_t avg_packed_qo_len, uint32_t head_dim) {
385	  if (avg_packed_qo_len > 64 && head_dim < 256) {
386	    return 128;
387	  } else {
388	    auto compute_capacity = GetCudaComputeCapability();
389	    if (compute_capacity.first >= 8) {
390	      // Ampere or newer
391	      if (avg_packed_qo_len > 16) {
392	        // avg_packed_qo_len <= 64
393	        return 64;
394	      } else {
395	        // avg_packed_qo_len <= 16
396	        return 16;
397	      }
398	    } else {
399	      // NOTE(Zihao): not enough shared memory on Turing for 1x4 warp layout
400	      return 64;
401	    }
402	  }
403	}
404	
405	inline int UpPowerOfTwo(int x) {
406	  // Returns the smallest power of two greater than or equal to x
407	  if (x <= 0) return 1;
408	  --x;
409	  x |= x >> 1;
```

330	        # Head group number derived from model configuration
331	        self.head_dim = model_runner.model_config.head_dim
332	        self.head_group_num = model_runner.model_config.num_key_value_heads
333	        self.heads_per_group = (
334	            model_runner.model_config.num_attention_heads // self.head_group_num
335	        )
336	        self.k1_kernel_size = self.kernel_size
337	        self.k1_kernel_stride = self.kernel_stride
338	        self.k2_kernel_size = self.kernel_size * 4
339	        self.k2_kernel_stride = self.kernel_stride * 4

# 128K Prefill 热点图

**测量日期**: 2026-04-20  
**模型**: MiniCPM-SALA-NVFP4（GPTQ46 + FourOverSix, calib90, 90K）  
**配置**: EAGLE-3 (spec_steps=1, topk=2, dtn=3), `--chunked-prefill-size 8192`, `--dense-as-sparse`, `minicpm_flashinfer`  
**请求**: `bench/data/speed_bench_c1.jsonl` 里 prompt_tokens=129719 的条目，`max_tokens=16`  
**端到端**: 14.6s / 请求（第二次 14.6s，几乎相同，已排除 compile / radix cache 效应）

## 采集方法

在三个模块加 `_profile_begin / _profile_end`（`torch.cuda.synchronize() + time.perf_counter()`）：

- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/models/minicpm.py` — 在 `MiniCPMDecoderLayer.forward` 里按层分桶（`attn_standard_ms` / `attn_gla_ms` / `mlp_ms` / `ln_pre_ms` / `ln_post_ms` / `residual_ms`），并在 `MiniCPMModel.forward` 出口按 per-forward 快照 diff 打印（`[minicpm-prefill-fwd]`）
- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_sparse_utils.py` — stage1 (`infllmv2_attn_stage1`)、`max_pooling_1d_varlen`、`block_score.topk` 各一桶
- `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/minicpm_backend.py` — `get_compress_k_v2` / `sparse_get_topk_impl` 外包一层；`attention_kernel.forward`（sparse path）外包一层（`extend_sparse_fa_ms`）

触发方式：`SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE=1 SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE_INTERVAL=32`。

注意：插桩强制 sync，破坏 CUDA kernel 重叠，绝对值偏高（cumulative 累积 ~19s vs 实际 prefill ~14s），**比例可信**。

## 每层占比（Chunk 1，token=8192，total=958ms）

| 阶段 | 时间 (ms) | 占比 |
|---|---|---|
| **extend_sparse_fa**（8 standard layer × sparse FA） | 251 | **26.2%** |
| **mlp**（32 层 MLP） | 151 | 15.8% |
| **attn_gla**（24 层 Lightning Attention） | 109 | 11.4% |
| attn_standard 其他（QKV proj + RoPE + out proj） | ~46 | 4.8% |
| sparse_topk 合计（stage1 + pool + topk_select） | 44 | 4.6% |
| &emsp;stage1_score (infllmv2_attn_stage1) | 28 | 2.9% |
| &emsp;topk_select (`block_score.topk`) | 11 | 1.2% |
| &emsp;max_pool (`max_pooling_1d_varlen`) | 3.6 | 0.4% |
| compress_k | 1.2 | 0.1% |
| ln_pre + ln_post + residual + embed + final_norm | ~16 | 1.7% |

注：`attn_standard` 包裹整个 `self.self_attn` 调用，里面的 sparse FA / sparse_topk / compress_k / QKV proj 已分别单独计时；`attn_standard_ms (342)` ≈ `extend_sparse_fa + sparse_topk + compress_k + QKV proj 等 = 251 + 44 + 1.2 + 46`。

## `attn_standard` 内部 (342ms) 拆分

| 子阶段 | 时间 (ms) | 占 attn_standard |
|---|---|---|
| extend_sparse_fa | 251 | **73%** |
| sparse_topk (s1+pool+topk) | 44 | 13% |
| QKV/OUT proj + RoPE 等 | ~46 | 14% |
| compress_k | 1.2 | 0.3% |

## 周期性 2× 慢 chunk（待调查）

每 3 个 chunk 就出现一次慢 chunk，`extend_sparse_fa` 和 `attn_standard` 翻一倍左右：

| chunk | total (ms) | attn_standard (ms) | extend_sparse_fa (ms) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 958 | 342 | 251 |
| 2 | 972 | 346 | 249 |
| **3** | **1670** | **692** | **591** |
| 4 | 998 | 355 | 248 |
| **5** | **1663** | 684 | 571 |
| 6 | 1029 | 364 | 247 |
| 7 | 1046 | 370 | 248 |
| **8** | **1720** | 705 | 577 |
| 9 | 1075 | 380 | 247 |
| 10 | 1090 | 385 | 247 |
| **11** | **1758** | 716 | 572 |
| 12 | 1122 | 396 | 247 |
| **13** | **1882** | 772 | 618 |
| 14 | (tail, 6839 tokens) | | |

周期性 ~2× 慢，差值稳定在 ~340ms（正好是一次 sparse FA 的时间，仿佛 "multi-pass" 或 "replay"）。可能候选：

- 稀疏 batch 分组边界 / dense fallback
- flashinfer prefill wrapper plan 重建（page layout 变化）
- CUDA graph / piecewise capture 边界
- 其他 K/V 重排 / scale 重建

这是**独立于 sparse 稀疏度**的开销（每 3 chunk 必然出现），单次慢 chunk 多 ~700ms × 5 次 ≈ **3.5s 额外 prefill**，相当于 128K 端到端 14.6s 的 **~24%**。

端到端潜在收益估算（若消除）：prefill 从 ~14s → ~10.5s，128K bench 请求 -24%。

## 后续方向

1. **A（先做）**：调查周期性 2× 慢 chunk 根因，消除非必要开销
2. **B**：针对 `extend_sparse_fa` 本身做 FlashPrefill α-threshold 改造（当前 fixed topk=96=64+32 local，改成 dynamic mask 可能 1.5-2× 加速，但要验证 accuracy 不退）
3. **C**：用 CUDA events (no-sync) 代替 profile_begin/end 拿到更准确的数字

## A 调查：周期性 2× 慢 chunk 根因（2026-04-20 已定位）

### 方法

在 `FlashInferKernel.forward` 把三个子步骤独立分桶：

- `fi_convert_ms` — `convert_sparse_page_table_to_flashinfer`
- `fi_begin_forward_ms` — `wrapper.begin_forward(...)` (== flashinfer `plan()`)
- `fi_decode_fwd_ms` — `wrapper.forward(...)` (实际 FA kernel)

在 `forward_extend` 里快照 diff 得到每个 standard layer 的三步时间，输出到 `[minicpm-prefill-fa-per-layer]`。

### 结论：慢点在 `wrapper.begin_forward` → flashinfer `plan()`

每个慢 chunk 恰有**一个** standard layer 的 `bf` 暴涨：
```

tool_result Bash
```
245 /user_4813494d/openbmb/docs/PREFILL-HOTMAP.md

| 场景 | cv (ms) | bf (ms) | fw (ms) | 总 (ms) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 正常 layer | 2.1 | 18.5-20 | 10.3 | ~31 |
| 慢 layer | 2.1 | **340-470** | 10.3 | **358-481** |

`cv` / `fw` 完全不变，`bf` 单峰 ~20×。慢 layer 在 {L9, L16, L17, L22, L29, L30} 之间无规律轮换，每 ~2-3 chunk 出现一次。

### plan() 内部做什么（flashinfer decode.py:825）

- CPU 侧小量 book-keeping: 拷贝 indptr_host / last_page_len_host（DtoH 同步）
- 调用 `get_batch_prefill_module(...)` 获取 `_cached_module`（首次触发 JIT，后续走 cache）
- `self._cached_module.plan(*args)` — C++ 做 work split 决定

### 最有可能的原因

1. **PyTorch caching allocator 抖动**：`plan()` 里每次做 `self._paged_kv_*_buf = tensor.to(device, non_blocking=True)` 会产生新的 tensor 引用替换旧的，旧的 tensor 放回 allocator cache。某种 fragmentation 边界下触发 `cudaMalloc`，此 driver 调用 300-500ms
2. **GPU allocator cache trim**（`torch.cuda.memory._record_memory_history` 可验证）

慢的 layer 位置随机、周期约 2-3 chunk 一次、跨请求 wheel 位置不同（L22 后见 L16 再后 L30...），与 allocator 抖动模式吻合。

### 影响

- 每慢层 ~350ms，每 chunk 出现 ~1 次
- 16 chunks × ~50% 出现率 × 350ms ≈ **2.8s / 请求**（占 14.6s 端到端 **19%**）
- 消除后 128K 请求理论从 14.6s → ~11.8s，约 -19%

### 待验证的修复方向

1. ~~**复用 kv_indptr / kv_indices / kv_last_page_len buffer**~~ — **已实验，无效**。改 FlashInferKernel 预分配 3 个 int32 buffer，每次复用 view，慢 layer 的频率 / 幅度都没变化。且关掉 profile 的 baseline 也是 14.5s，和带 profile 完全一样——说明 stall 在 real walltime 里客观存在，不是 profile sync 造成的假象，也不是 allocator 抖动
2. **nsys profile** — 下一步最有价值。抓一次 128K prefill 的 Nsight Systems trace，定位慢的 350ms gap 里 GPU 在干什么（空闲？单个 kernel 执行？driver API 调用？）。用 `nsys profile -t cuda,nvtx,cudnn -o trace.qdrep python3 -m sglang.launch_server ...`
3. **CUDA graph 包 sparse prefill 的 begin_forward + forward** — 难度高，plan 是 eager mode 操作
4. **flashinfer sparse API 绕过 decode wrapper** — 直接调 BlockSparse / VariableLength 的 plan-less attention
5. **只在第 1 个 standard layer 调 plan，后续 layer 复用 plan_info 但更新 indices buffer 内容**（plan 的 split 策略依赖于 kv_lens 和 bs，这些跨 layer 不变；只 `_paged_kv_indices_buf` 内容变）
6. **自写 sparse FA kernel** 替代 flashinfer decode wrapper 路径（大改）

### 已撤回的修改

`FlashInferKernel.__init__` 里的 `_plan_kv_*_buf` 已移除；`forward` 里 `torch.zeros` 回到原版。profile 插桩（`fi_convert_ms` / `fi_begin_forward_ms` / `fi_decode_fwd_ms` / `extend_sparse_fa_ms` / `stage1_score_prefill_ms` 等、`[minicpm-prefill-fwd]` / `[minicpm-prefill-fa-per-layer]` 两个 print）保留，默认靠 `SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE=1` 关闭，复查时可用。

## A 调查续：nsys trace 定位 plan() 慢因（2026-04-20）

### trace 抓取

在 `MiniCPMModel.forward` 里加了 `cudaProfilerStart/Stop` 控制窗口（`SGLANG_MINICPM_CUDA_PROFILER=1`，默认抓 4 个 chunk），并在 `FlashInferKernel.forward` / `minicpm_backend.forward_extend` 里加 NVTX range（`SGLANG_MINICPM_NVTX=1`）。

用 `nsys profile -t cuda,nvtx --capture-range=cudaProfilerApi --capture-range-end=stop` 抓到 `/tmp/nsys_128k.nsys-rep`（762KB，4 chunk × 8 standard layer = 32 次 bf 调用，其中 1 次 L22 是 357ms 的 outlier）。

### 关键发现：bf 几乎全是 CPU 时间

**Normal L0 (24ms) 窗口内时序**（从 bf 起点算起）：

| t (ms) | 事件 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 0.07 | cudaMemcpyAsync (12us) | 小 HtoD |
| 0.09 | cudaMemcpyAsync (332us) | 64KB HtoD 的 indptr |
| 0.42 | cudaStreamSynchronize (3us) | sync |
| 0.43 | cudaMemcpyAsync (13us) | 小 DtoH |
| 0.44 | cudaStreamSynchronize (2us) | sync |
| 0.45-23.84 | **CPU 空白 23.4ms**，无任何 kernel / API / memcpy | |
| 23.84 | cudaMemcpyAsync (19us, 256KB DtoH) | plan 输出 |

**Slow L22 (357ms) 窗口内时序**：

| t (ms) | 事件 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 0.02 | cudaMemcpyAsync (8us) | 小 HtoD |
| 0.03 | cudaMemcpyAsync (**4550us**) | 64KB HtoD **异常慢 14×** |
| 0.5-4.6 | 一连串 kernel（elementwise, cumsum, reduce, scan, flatten_and_fill） | plan 内部 GPU 辅助计算（normal 窗口没有） |
| 4.6-357.5 | **CPU 空白 353ms**，无任何 kernel / API / memcpy | |
| 357.5 | cudaMemcpyAsync (49us, 256KB DtoH) | plan 输出 |

### 结论改写

1. **plan() 的 23ms baseline 是纯 CPU 时间** — 不是 GPU/driver 慢，而是 flashinfer `_cached_module.plan(...)` C++ 函数在做 work-split 决策的 CPU 计算。16 chunk × 8 layer × 23ms = **2.9s / 请求**，占 14.6s 的 **~20%** —— 这是**基线开销**，和慢 outlier 无关。
2. **Slow 慢层的 353ms gap 也是纯 CPU** — 同一个 CPU 计算偶尔暴涨 17×。配套 signal: 开头有一个异常慢的 HtoD（4.55ms vs 0.33ms normal）+ plan 内部额外跑了一串 GPU 辅助 kernel。说明 CPU 这次分支走了"重计算路径"，且开头 memcpy 被什么东西阻塞了（可能是 ioctl / allocator / page fault）。
3. **与 allocator 抖动无关** — nsys 窗口内没看到 cudaMalloc/cudaFree（只在 init 阶段有 11 次 cudaMalloc），证实 buffer reuse 实验为什么没用。

### 真实优化空间

**baseline 2.9s + outlier 2.8s ≈ 5.7s / 请求**，是 14.6s 的 **39%**。这比之前估计的 19% 高得多。

### 修复优先级重排

1. **（强推）layer 间复用 plan_info** — 同一个 forward 的 8 个 standard layer 的 KV layout 完全一致（max_kv_len、bs、page_size 都相同），只有 `_paged_kv_indices` 内容不同。如能让 8 层共用一次 plan()，单 chunk 省 7 × 23ms = **161ms**；16 chunk 省 **2.6s**。这是最大单笔收益。
2. **（较强）chunk 间复用 plan_info** — chunk 间 max_kv_len 在增长但 bs 固定=1，plan 的 split 策略对增量 kv_len 多数情况不变。缓存最近一次 plan 的 split decision，在 kv_len 小幅变化时跳过 replan。若有效再省 50-70%。
3. **（高风险）自己实现 sparse decode kernel** — 直接绕过 flashinfer，用 Triton/CUTLASS 写个 block-sparse FA。但 plan() 里的 work split 逻辑也要重写。
4. ~~buffer 复用~~（已验证无效）
5. ~~CUDA graph 包 bf+fw~~（plan 是 eager C++，graph 捕不了）

### 修复 1：layer 间复用 plan_info（2026-04-20 已落地）

**实现**：`FlashInferKernel.__init__` 新增 `_plan_cache_key` / `_plan_last_layer_id`。`forward()` 的非 CUDA-graph + 非 prefill（sparse decode wrapper）分支里，判定 `layer_id > _plan_last_layer_id` 且 cache_key 匹配则跳过 `wrapper.begin_forward`，直接 `wrapper._paged_kv_indices_buf = kv_indices`（flashinfer plan 依赖的 batch_size / indptr / last_page_len / heads / dtypes 在同一 forward 的 8 个 standard layer 里完全相同，只有 indices 内容变化）。关键是 cache 失效逻辑：下一次 forward 从 L0 开始 `0 > 31 == False` 自动触发 replan。env var `SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE=0` 可关。

**profile 验证**（SGLANG_MINICPM_PROFILE=1 per-chunk FA log）：

```
chunk: L0=32.2ms[bf=19.6] L9=12.3ms[bf=0.0] L16=12.2ms[bf=0.0] L17=12.2ms[bf=0.0]
       L22=11.8ms[bf=0.0] L29=11.8ms[bf=0.0] L30=12.3ms[bf=0.0] L31=12.3ms[bf=0.0]
```
```

**端到端实测**（129955-token 请求，max_tokens=8）：

| 版本 | e2e |

| plan cache | **10.3-10.6s** |
| 节省 | **-28~29%** |

**correctness**：短 chat + 130K 两条请求输出均为正常中文 / 合法推理，无 NaN / 乱码。

**decode 无影响**：plan cache 代码在 `FlashInferKernel.forward` 的 non-CUDA-graph 分支，decode 走 CUDA graph 分支（`params.decode_wrapper is not None`）物理隔离。实测 cache on/off 的 decode TPS 在噪声级别一致（146.8 vs 147.7, 129.8 vs 129.9）。

### 修复 2：chunk 间 plan_info 复用（2026-04-20 已落地）

**动机**：layer-cache 落地后，每 chunk 第 1 个 standard layer（L0）仍触发一次 replan（20ms/次 × 16 chunks = 320ms），占剩余可优化空间的大头。观察到 InfLLM-v2 在 seq_len 超过 topk 阈值后，`kv_indptr`、`kv_last_page_len`、bs、page_size 都固定；只有 `kv_indices` 随 chunk 推进变化，而 flashinfer `plan()` 的 work-split 完全由前面三个量决定 —— 跨 chunk 同样可复用。

**实现**：cache 结构新增 `_plan_cached_indptr_bytes` / `_plan_cached_lpl_bytes`（CPU-side 字节副本）。当 layer 级别判定失败（forward 边界 / L0），进一步做 CPU byte compare：若 `kv_indptr` 和 `kv_last_page_len` 与缓存一致，复用 plan；否则 replan + 刷新缓存。bs ≤ 16 时这两个张量 total ≤ ~256B，`.cpu().numpy().tobytes()` 开销可忽略。

**profile 验证**（128K 请求多 chunk log）：
```
chunk 3+: L0=12.4ms[cv=2.1,bf=0.1,fw=10.2]    # cross-hit: bf 从 20ms → 0.1ms
chunk 1:  L0=32.0ms[bf=20ms]                   # true miss: seq_len < full topk, indptr 结构变
```
多数 chunk cross-hit（~14/16），仅早期 seq_len 未达 full topk 阈值的 chunk 仍 replan。

### Smax=64 纯 prefill A/B 验证（2026-04-20）

`bench/prefill_bench_smax64.py`：64 个 smax 样本（prompt_tokens min=25 / p50=49.7K / max=136.5K）并发全发，`max_tokens=1`，服务启动前 flush_cache，隔离掉 decode 影响。

| 配置 | total | p50 latency | max latency |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE=0`（off） | 416.29s | 60.12s | 416.26s |
| **cache on（layer+chunk）** | **290.23s** | **41.90s** | 290.20s |
| **加速** | **-30.3%** | **-30.3%** | — |

结论：plan cache 在并发长序列场景下稳定省 **~30% 纯 prefill 时间**，p50 / max latency 同步下降。

### 下一步

- **L0 残留 replan**：chunk 1 仍 replan（kv_indptr 结构因 topk 不足而变化）。可尝试把 kv_indptr 刻意 pad 到 full topk，或把 L0 的 20ms 转成 CUDA kernel 之外 overlap。收益 ~2% e2e。
- **plan() 偶发 350ms spike**：1/chunk 概率、非 layer 数线性，可能源自 flashinfer 内部 autotune 分配。关注中。
- **自写 sparse decode kernel 绕过 flashinfer**：彻底解决 plan() 但风险高，延后。
- **共享其他 cache 字段**：除 `_paged_kv_indices_buf` 外还有 `_paged_kv_last_page_len_buf`（已在 begin_forward 里重写），当前 cross-hit 路径只改 indices；若确认 lpl 无需重写可进一步减分支。

  "old_string": "        self.update_batch_for_sparse(forward_batch, metadata)\n\n        self.forward_metadata = metadata\n\n    def get_topk_for_sparse(",
  "new_string": "        self.update_batch_for_sparse(forward_batch, metadata)\n\n        # Preallocate compressed_k / compressed_k2 -inf buffers once per forward.\n        # get_topk_for_sparse is called per standard attention layer (8 layers for\n        # SALA), and the previous impl did torch.full(-inf) per-layer (~16MB + 4MB\n        # bf16 fill). Layers only write slots [0, n_compressed), so slots beyond\n        # retain -inf across layers within the same forward. Re-fill once per\n        # forward to erase the previous request's residual.\n        if (\n            forward_batch.forward_mode.is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed()\n            and getattr(forward_batch, \"sparse_batch_size\", 0) == forward_batch.batch_size\n            and forward_batch.sparse_batch_size > 0\n        ):\n            bs = forward_batch.batch_size\n            head_group = self.head_group_num\n            head_dim = self.head_dim\n            k1_rows = bs * self.max_context_len // self.k1_kernel_stride\n            k2_rows = bs * self.max_context_len // self.k2_kernel_stride\n            shape1 = (k1_rows, head_group, head_dim)\n            shape2 = (k2_rows, head_group, head_dim)\n            neg_inf = float(\"-inf\")\n            if self._compressed_k_buf is None or tuple(self._compressed_k_buf.shape) != shape1:\n                self._compressed_k_buf = torch.full(\n                    shape1, dtype=self.params_dtype, device=self.device, fill_value=neg_inf\n                )\n            else:\n                self._compressed_k_buf.fill_(neg_inf)\n            if self._compressed_k2_buf is None or tuple(self._compressed_k2_buf.shape) != shape2:\n                self._compressed_k2_buf = torch.full(\n                    shape2, dtype=self.params_dtype, device=self.device, fill_value=neg_inf\n                )\n            else:\n                self._compressed_k2_buf.fill_(neg_inf)\n            metadata.compressed_k_buf = self._compressed_k_buf\n            metadata.compressed_k2_buf = self._compressed_k2_buf\n\n        self.forward_metadata = metadata\n\n    def get_topk_for_sparse("
}

在这个工作中 给我讲讲你做了什么工作 预期有什么收效？

> AGENT

## 这一轮工作总结

### 做了什么

**1. 跑 nsys profile 定位真实瓶颈**（Task #19, #20）
启动 EAGLE-3 server（spec_steps=1 topk=2 dtn=3），nsys 抓 150s 工作负载（1 个 130K prefill + 1 个 500-decode 请求）。生成 GPU kernel 总表归类：

| 类别 | ms | % |
|---|---|---|
| CUTLASS NVFP4 GEMM | 3278 | **35.9** |
| InfLLM-v2 stage2（BatchPrefill dim=16） | 1199 | **13.1** |
| Std Attention dim=128 | 999 | 11.0 |
| **Fill/Copy/Add 散小算子** | **1004** | **11.0**（33k calls） |
| InfLLM-v2 stage1 + support | 1088 | 11.9 |
| RMSNorm + SiLU + quantize | 870 | 9.5 |

**2. 排查 InfLLM-v2 stage2**
读 flashinfer KernelTraits 模板参数 + SALA sparse_bs 构造逻辑，算出 stage2 是 L2 bound、~30% 算力利用率，已经接近 memory-bound 天花板 → 难啃。

**3. 定位到一个干净的小胜场并实现**
Profile 里 **FillFunctor<bf16> 225ms / 843 calls = 267us 均值**。读代码定位到 `minicpm_backend.py:695-706`：sparse prefill 路径每个 standard attention layer 都 `torch.full((bs*524288/16, 2, 128), -inf)` 创建 16MB + 4MB 的 compressed_k / compressed_k2 缓冲。**8 层 × 16 chunk = 128 次，每次重新 alloc + fill -inf**。

实现**跨层复用**：
- `__init__` 加 `_compressed_k_buf / _compressed_k2_buf = None`
- `init_forward_metadata`：per-forward alloc 一次 + `.fill_(-inf)` 一次，挂到 metadata 上
- `get_topk_for_sparse` 的 all_sparse 分支：删 torch.full，改读 `metadata.compressed_k_buf`

正确性依据：compress_k triton kernel 只 tl.store 进 `[0, n_compressed)` 槽位，其余保持 -inf；layer 写入后立即被本层消费，层间互不污染；跨 forward 由 init_forward_metadata 重 fill 兜底。

### 预期收效

**下界**：225ms fill 减 7/8 → 省 ~197ms GPU 时间 ≈ **2.5% e2e**。

186:### 3.6 Prefill 加速（新增方向，重要但未量化）
188:**Prefill 占比不可忽略**：bench 数据集 prompt 长度分布极端偏长：
196:一半样本 prompt >32K，10% 样本 prompt >130K。对于 completion ~1K 的请求，prefill 占墙钟 >60%；对于 completion ~30K 的长输出（极少数样本），decode 主导。**平均而言 prefill 至少占 30-50% 端到端时间**，是 decode 之外唯一的大块。
198:**当前 prefill 路径**：8 层 standard attention 走 InfLLM-v2 稀疏（`minicpm_sparse_utils.py`，三阶段：compress_k → stage1 block_score → stage2 top-K sparse FA），24 层 GLA 线性 O(N)，总体已比 full-dense 快，但还有空间。
200:#### 3.6.1 借鉴 FlashPrefill (~/FlashPrefill) 调研
202:FlashPrefill 是 MInference / FlexPrefill 改进版，提供三个与 InfLLM-v2 可直接对比的技术点：
205:- 当前 InfLLM-v2: **固定 top-K blocks**，所有 query 无差别算同数量 blocks
206:- FlashPrefill: `score ≥ α × max_score` + `min_budget` 保底 + sink/window/last-N-full 保护
209:- 风险：中高；首先 profile 看 InfLLM-v2 实际 block 选择分布是否真的"过度"
212:- InfLLM-v2 用 `compress_k_core_new`（可训练 pooling kernel，输出 k1+k2）+ `infllmv2_attn_stage1`（两阶段 softmax attention）
213:- FlashPrefill 用 `compute_mean_vector`（纯 mean）+ `compute_block_score`（直接 QK^T + 双层 max reduce）
218:- FlashPrefill 的 block-sparse FA kernel 做了 (Q_TILE×K_TILE) × (warps×stages) 多维 autotune
219:- InfLLM-v2 stage2 kernel 可以借用这个 config 空间重新调优（低风险）
221:#### 3.6.2 行动路线

224:- [ ] **Step 2 (Microbench)**：单独测 FlashPrefill `compute_mean_vector + compute_block_score` vs MiniCPM `compressed_attention` 在我们的 shape 上的时延，head_dim=128, seq∈{16K, 64K, 128K}。

229:**备注**：FlashPrefill 原生 targets vLLM + HF attention，移植到 SGLang 要接 `minicpm_backend` 的 SparseMetadata + paged cache。Step 1-3 是低风险离线验证，Step 4-5 需要完整 e2e bench + accuracy。

查到了，之前有非常详尽的**同类优化**工作记录：

### 之前做过的 (docs/PREFILL-HOTMAP.md, commit e99478e + 259b82d)

**目标**：flashinfer `plan()` 的 CPU work-split（per-layer per-chunk 20ms）

**实现**：
- **修复 1**: 8 个 standard layer 共享一次 `plan()` → 省 161ms/chunk
- **修复 2**: chunk 间 byte-compare indptr/lpl 跨 chunk 复用（14/16 cross-hit）

**收效**：
- 130K 请求 14.6s → **10.3s (-29%)**
- Smax=64 纯 prefill 416s → **290s (-30.3%)**

### 我这次做的

**目标**：compressed_k/k2 buffer 的 `torch.full(-inf)` 每 layer 16MB + 4MB fill

**原则完全一致**（per-layer 重复工作 → per-forward 摊销），但对象不同：
- 之前攻 **flashinfer plan()**（20ms × 128 次 = 2.6s 可省）
- 这次攻 **compressed_k fill**（267us × 256 次 = 200ms 可省）

**量级差 10×**（2.5% vs 30%），因为 plan() 的 per-call 成本比 fill 重两个数量级。

|---|---|---|

| 3.6.1 Step 2 | microbench FlashPrefill compute_block_score vs MiniCPM compressed_attention | 判断 Step 5 价值 |
| 3.6.1 Step 5 | 用 FlashPrefill `_flash_forward` 替换 stage2（需适配 paged KV + varlen） | 大饼 |

plan cache 套路可比你更省吧 你仔细看看你的实现思路和他是不是对齐了？

> AGENT

仔细看了下，plan cache 套路是 **cross-layer + cross-chunk 两层复用**，我当前只做了 cross-layer，没做 cross-chunk。**本质没对齐**。

### 对比

| | plan cache | 我现在 |
|---|---|---|
| 跨 layer 复用 | ✓（layer_id > last_layer_id 就 skip） | ✓（init 一次共享 8 层） |
| 跨 chunk 复用 | ✓（byte compare indptr/lpl，14/16 chunk cross-hit） | **✗ 每 chunk 都 fill 一次** |

### 我的漏洞

SALA chunked prefill：chunk i 写 compressed_k[0, N_i)，chunk i+1 写 [0, N_{i+1}) 且 N_{i+1} > N_i（严格扩张）。chunk i 的写入会被 chunk i+1 **完全覆盖**，slots [N_{i+1}, MAX) 仍保留初始 -inf。**同一请求跨 chunk 根本不需要 refill**。

我当前每 chunk 都 `fill_(-inf)`，浪费 15/16 次。量化：
- 当前省：256 fills → 16 fills（每请求 16 chunks × 2 buffers）
- 改对后：16 → 1（每请求只首 chunk fill）
- 额外省：15 × 2 × 267us ≈ **8ms/请求**，0.07% e2e（小，但对齐套路）

### 失效判据

只需判"新请求 vs 同请求续 chunk"：
- bs=1 + `extend_prefix_lens_cpu[0] == 0` → 新请求 → fill
- bs=1 + `extend_prefix_lens_cpu[0] > 0` → 续 chunk → skip
- bs>1 → 保守 fill（同 plan cache 对 byte-compare miss 的处理）

改？改的话 plan cache 套路就对齐了，尽管绝对收益很小（毕竟 fill 本来就便宜）。

9	def compress_k_complete_kernel_new(
10	    key_cache_ptr,
11	    token_table_ptr,
12	    cu_new_k_token_nums_ptr,
13	    history_compress_k_token_nums_ptr,
14	    k_stride,

21	    max_chunks_per_seq,
22	    token_table_cols,
23	    compressed_k_table_cols,
24	    head_num_k: tl.constexpr,

27	    kernel_stride: tl.constexpr,

29	    max_grid_chunks: tl.constexpr,
30	 ):
31	    """
32	    Single-kernel implementation that fuses k computation, key compression,
33	    key_cache write, and full_compressed_k read for ALL chunks (history + new).
34	
35	    Grid: (batch_size, min(max_total_chunks, max_grid_chunks), head_num_k)
36	    where max_total_chunks = max_chunks_per_seq + max_history_chunks
37	    - chunk_in_seq in [0, history_chunks_in_seq): process HISTORY chunks
38	    - chunk_in_seq in [history_chunks_in_seq, total_chunks_in_seq): process NEW chunks
39	    
40	    If total_chunks > max_grid_chunks, each thread block loops to handle multiple chunks.
41	
42	    Each thread processes one (batch, chunk_in_seq, head) combination.
43	    Only head=0 threads write to key_cache or full_compressed_k to avoid redundant writes.
44	
45	    Args:
46	        key_cache_ptr: Input key cache tensor [total_tokens, head_num_k, head_dim]
47	        token_table_ptr: Token table [batch_size, token_table_cols]
48	        cu_new_k_token_nums_ptr: Cumulative new token nums [batch_size + 1]
49	        history_compress_k_token_nums_ptr: History compressed token nums [batch_size]
50	        k_stride: Stride for k computation
51	        compressed_k_table_ptr: Compressed k table [batch_size, compressed_k_table_cols]
52	        cu_new_compress_k_token_nums_ptr: Cumulative new compressed token nums [batch_size + 1]
53	        cu_total_compress_k_token_nums_ptr: Cumulative total compressed token nums [batch_size + 1]
54	        total_compress_k_token_nums_ptr: Total compressed token nums per batch [batch_size]
55	        full_compressed_k_ptr: Output buffer [total_compressed_tokens, head_num_k, head_dim]
56	        batch_size: Number of sequences in batch
57	        max_chunks_per_seq: Maximum possible NEW chunks per sequence
58	        token_table_cols: Number of columns in token_table
59	        compressed_k_table_cols: Number of columns in compressed_k_table
60	        head_num_k: Number of attention heads
61	        head_dim: Dimension per head
62	        kernel_size: Tokens per chunk for compression
63	        kernel_stride: Stride between chunk starts
64	        BLOCK_SIZE: Vectorized load/store width
65	        max_grid_chunks: Maximum grid dimension for chunks (kernel loops if more chunks needed)
66	    """
67	    batch_idx = tl.program_id(0)
68	    grid_chunk_idx = tl.program_id(1)
69	    head_idx = tl.program_id(2)
70	    
71	    # Total number of chunks this thread block needs to process
72	    chunk_stride = max_grid_chunks
73	
74	    if batch_idx >= batch_size or head_idx >= head_num_k:
75	        return
76	
77	    # ====================================================================
78	    # PHASE 0: Determine chunk type and boundaries
79	    # ====================================================================
80	
81	    history_compress = tl.load(history_compress_k_token_nums_ptr + batch_idx)
82	
83	    # Compute how many NEW chunks this sequence actually has
84	    cu_new_k_start = tl.load(cu_new_k_token_nums_ptr + batch_idx)
85	    cu_new_k_end = tl.load(cu_new_k_token_nums_ptr + batch_idx + 1)
86	    new_k_count = cu_new_k_end - cu_new_k_start
87	    new_chunks_in_seq = tl.where(
88	        new_k_count >= kernel_size,
89	        (new_k_count - kernel_size) // kernel_stride + 1,
90	        0
91	    )
92	
93	    # Total chunks = history + new
94	    history_chunks_in_seq = history_compress
95	    total_chunks_in_seq = history_chunks_in_seq + new_chunks_in_seq
96	
97	    # Get cumulative positions for this batch
98	    cu_total_start = tl.load(cu_total_compress_k_token_nums_ptr + batch_idx)
99	
100	    # ====================================================================
101	    # LOOP: Handle multiple chunks per thread block if needed
102	    # ====================================================================
103	    
104	    # Iterate over all chunks assigned to this thread block
105	    chunk_in_seq = grid_chunk_idx
106	    
107	    while chunk_in_seq < total_chunks_in_seq:
108	        # Determine if processing history or new chunks
109	        is_history_chunk = chunk_in_seq < history_chunks_in_seq
110	
111	        if is_history_chunk:
112	            # ====================================================================
113	            # PHASE 1: Process HISTORY chunks
114	            # ====================================================================
115	
116	            # chunk_in_seq in [0, history_compress) -> history chunk index
117	            history_chunk_idx = chunk_in_seq
118	
119	            # Compute output position in full_compressed_k: cu_total_start + history_chunk_idx

> AGENT

读 stage1 kernel 签名确认了：`max_seqlen_k` 是 k1_cache_len（max_context 推的）而非 current k1 length。stage1 **会读到 buffer 尾部 tail**，依赖 -inf mask → fill 必要。

**正确性推导**（我检查了 compress_k kernel 源）：
- 写入严格 bounded 到 `[0, total_chunks_in_seq) = [0, history + new)`
- 跨 chunk 同请求：N 严格扩张（history 从 chunk i 的 compressed count 开始），chunk i+1 完全覆盖 chunk i 的 slots，尾部 -inf 保留 ✓
- 跨请求（新请求）：N 可能 shrink → 尾部有旧请求残留 → 必须 refill ✓

**判据**：bs=1 + 同 req_pool_idx + `extend_prefix_lens == last_total_len` → 续 chunk，skip fill。其他情况 fill。

Reused across all standard attention layers AND across chunks of\n        # the same request (plan-cache-style: cross-layer + cross-chunk). Refill\n        # with -inf only on realloc, first call, or request switch.\n        #\n        # Correctness: stage1 reads slots up to k1_cache_len (= max_context / stride),\n        # relying on -inf beyond cu_seqlens[-1] for masking. Between requests the total length may shrink, which could\n        # expose the previous request's residual K before the new -inf tail —\n        # so a full refill is required on request-switch.\n        self._compressed_k_buf = None\n        self._compressed_k2_buf = None\n        self._last_req_sig: Optional[Tuple[int, int]] = None  # (req_pool_idx, total_len)"

{

  "old_string": "        self.update_batch_for_sparse(forward_batch, metadata)\n\n        # Preallocate compressed_k / compressed_k2 -inf buffers once per forward.\n        # get_topk_for_sparse is called per standard attention layer (8 layers for\n        # SALA), and the previous impl did torch.full(-inf) per-layer (~16MB + 4MB\n        # bf16 fill). Layers only write slots [0, n_compressed), so slots beyond\n        # retain -inf across layers within the same forward. Re-fill once per\n        # forward to erase the previous request's residual.\n        if (\n            forward_batch.forward_mode.is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed()\n            and getattr(forward_batch, \"sparse_batch_size\", 0) == forward_batch.batch_size\n            and forward_batch.sparse_batch_size > 0\n        ):\n            bs = forward_batch.batch_size\n            head_group = self.head_group_num\n            head_dim = self.head_dim\n            k1_rows = bs * self.max_context_len // self.k1_kernel_stride\n            k2_rows = bs * self.max_context_len // self.k2_kernel_stride\n            shape1 = (k1_rows, head_group, head_dim)\n            shape2 = (k2_rows, head_group, head_dim)\n            neg_inf = float(\"-inf\")\n            if self._compressed_k_buf is None or tuple(self._compressed_k_buf.shape) != shape1:\n                self._compressed_k_buf = torch.full(\n                    shape1, dtype=self.params_dtype, device=self.device, fill_value=neg_inf\n                )\n            else:\n                self._compressed_k_buf.fill_(neg_inf)\n            if self._compressed_k2_buf is None or tuple(self._compressed_k2_buf.shape) != shape2:\n                self._compressed_k2_buf = torch.full(\n                    shape2, dtype=self.params_dtype, device=self.device, fill_value=neg_inf\n                )\n            else:\n                self._compressed_k2_buf.fill_(neg_inf)\n            metadata.compressed_k_buf = self._compressed_k_buf\n            metadata.compressed_k2_buf = self._compressed_k2_buf\n\n        self.forward_metadata = metadata",
  "new_string": "        self.update_batch_for_sparse(forward_batch, metadata)\n\n        # Plan-cache-style buffer reuse for compressed_k / compressed_k2:\n        #   cross-layer — share across 8 standard attention layers in one forward\n        #   cross-chunk — skip refill when the current call is a continuation of\n        #                 the same request (extend_prefix_lens == last total_len).\n        # Refill only fires on: first call, shape change (bs flip), or request\n        # switch. See __init__ for correctness reasoning.\n        if (\n            forward_batch.forward_mode.is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed()\n            and getattr(forward_batch, \"sparse_batch_size\", 0) == forward_batch.batch_size\n            and forward_batch.sparse_batch_size > 0\n        ):\n            bs = forward_batch.batch_size\n            k1_rows = bs * self.max_context_len // self.k1_kernel_stride\n            k2_rows = bs * self.max_context_len // self.k2_kernel_stride\n            shape1 = (k1_rows, self.head_group_num, self.head_dim)\n            shape2 = (k2_rows, self.head_group_num, self.head_dim)\n            neg_inf = float(\"-inf\")\n\n            realloc = (\n                self._compressed_k_buf is None\n                or tuple(self._compressed_k_buf.shape) != shape1\n                or tuple(self._compressed_k2_buf.shape) != shape2\n            )\n            if realloc:\n                self._compressed_k_buf = torch.full(\n                    shape1, dtype=self.params_dtype, device=self.device, fill_value=neg_inf\n                )\n                self._compressed_k2_buf = torch.full(\n                    shape2, dtype=self.params_dtype, device=self.device, fill_value=neg_inf\n                )\n                self._last_req_sig = None\n\n            # Cross-chunk continuation only handled for bs=1 (common long-prefill\n            # case). bs>1 falls back to always-refill for safety.\n            is_continuation = False\n            if bs == 1 and self._last_req_sig is not None:\n                cur_req_idx = int(forward_batch.req_pool_indices[0].item())\n                cur_prefix = int(forward_batch.extend_prefix_lens_cpu[0])\n                last_req_idx, last_total_len = self._last_req_sig\n                is_continuation = (\n                    cur_req_idx == last_req_idx and cur_prefix == last_total_len\n                )\n\n            if not realloc and not is_continuation:\n                # Request switch or first call post-realloc-miss: refill.\n                self._compressed_k_buf.fill_(neg_inf)\n                self._compressed_k2_buf.fill_(neg_inf)\n\n            # Stash signature for next call.\n            if bs == 1:\n                cur_req_idx = int(forward_batch.req_pool_indices[0].item())\n                cur_prefix = int(forward_batch.extend_prefix_lens_cpu[0])\n                cur_seq = int(forward_batch.extend_seq_lens_cpu[0])\n                self._last_req_sig = (cur_req_idx, cur_prefix + cur_seq)\n            else:\n                self._last_req_sig = None\n\n            metadata.compressed_k_buf = self._compressed_k_buf\n            metadata.compressed_k2_buf = self._compressed_k2_buf\n\n        self.forward_metadata = metadata"
}

"""Pure-prefill bench: 64 smax samples with max_tokens=1.

Measures total wall-clock to prefill all 64 requests sent in parallel
(no concurrency cap, like Smax). Each request forces 1 output token so
near all time is spent in prefill path — used to validate the plan-cache
optimization in isolation.

Usage: python3 bench/prefill_bench_smax64.py [N_TRIALS]
"""
import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import os
import sys
import time

import httpx

DATA = "/user_4813494d/openbmb/bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl"
API = os.getenv("BENCH_API", "http://127.0.0.1:30000")
TIMEOUT = 600.0
MAX_TOKENS = 1


async def flush_cache(client):
    try:
        await client.post(f"{API}/flush_cache", timeout=30)
    except Exception:
        pass


async def one_request(client, idx, prompt, sem):
    async with sem:
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        r = await client.post(
            f"{API}/v1/chat/completions",
            json={
                "model": "minicpm",
                "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                "max_tokens": MAX_TOKENS,
```

tool_use Bash

  "description": "Rest of bench script"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result Bash
```
"max_tokens": MAX_TOKENS,
                "temperature": 0.0,
            },
            timeout=TIMEOUT,
        )
        dt = time.perf_counter() - t0
        ok = r.status_code == 200
        usage = r.json().get("usage", {}) if ok else {}
        return {
            "idx": idx,
            "ok": ok,
            "latency_s": dt,
            "prompt_tokens": usage.get("prompt_tokens", -1),
            "completion_tokens": usage.get("completion_tokens", -1),
            "status": r.status_code,
        }


async def trial(items, concurrency):
    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(concurrency)
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        await flush_cache(client)
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        results = await asyncio.gather(
            *[one_request(client, it["index"], it["question"], sem) for it in items]
        )
        total = time.perf_counter() - t0
    return total, results


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=2)
    ap.add_argument("--concurrency", type=int, default=64)
    ap.add_argument("--tag", type=str, default="")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    items = [json.loads(l) for l in open(DATA)]
    print(f"loaded {len(items)} samples, api={API}, concurrency={args.concurrency}")
    print(f"SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE={os.getenv('SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE', '1(default)')}")
    print(f"tag={args.tag!r}")

    totals = []
    for t in range(args.trials):
        total, results = asyncio.run(trial(items, args.concurrency))
        n_ok = sum(1 for r in results if r["ok"])
        lats = sorted(r["latency_s"] for r in results if r["ok"])
        p50 = lats[len(lats)//2] if lats else 0
        p99 = lats[int(len(lats)*0.99)] if lats else 0
        mx = lats[-1] if lats else 0
        totals.append(total)
        print(
            f"[trial {t+1}] total={total:.2f}s  ok={n_ok}/{len(results)}  "
            f"p50={p50:.2f}s p99={p99:.2f}s max={mx:.2f}s"
        )

    if totals:
        avg = sum(totals) / len(totals)
        print(f"== avg total = {avg:.2f}s  (n_trials={len(totals)})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

> AGENT

A/B 同 session 结果：

| | trial 1 | trial 2 | avg |
|---|---|---|---|
| reuse **ON** | 295.73s | 291.80s | **293.76s** |
| reuse **OFF** | 292.43s | 291.81s | **292.12s** |

**Delta: +1.64s (ON 反而慢 0.56%)**，在两 trial 噪声内。compressed_k 复用**无可测收益**。

原因推测：
- fill 200ms/请求大概率和前面的 kernel overlap，不在 critical path
- torch caching allocator 让重复同尺寸 `torch.full` 几乎零开销
- smax=64 下 per-request 小优化被互相排队延迟吞没

先确认 130K 单请求场景是否也无收益（之前 11.0s vs 11.31s 可能是 nsys overhead 的差）。

[1] e2e=11.34s  prompt=129955 completion=128  out='<think>  根据提供的搜索结果，可以推断出以下信息：  1.  **电影《巴山夜雨》**：     *   这部电'
[2] e2e=10.95s  prompt=129955 completion=128  out='<think>  根据提供的搜索结果，可以推断出以下信息：  1.  **电影《巴山夜雨》**：     *   这部电'
```

> AGENT

130K 单请求 A/B：
- reuse OFF: 11.12s (avg of 11.08/11.34/10.95)
- reuse ON: 11.02s (earlier)

Delta 0.9%，单轮方差 0.13s 就能抹平。**确认无可测收益**。

结论：revert。代码复杂但零收益，保留没意义。

+        # Preallocated compressed_k / compressed_k2 buffers for the sparse prefill
+        # path. Reused across all standard attention layers AND across chunks of

+        # with -inf only on realloc, first call, or request switch.
+        #
+        # Correctness: stage1 reads slots up to k1_cache_len (= max_context / stride),
+        # relying on -inf beyond cu_seqlens[-1] for masking. Writes are strictly
+        # bounded to [0, total_chunks_in_seq). Within the same request chunks
+        # extend strictly forward, so the "-inf tail" past the current end is
+        # preserved.  Between requests the total length may shrink, which could
+        # expose the previous request's residual K before the new -inf tail —
+        # so a full refill is required on request-switch.
+        self._compressed_k_buf = None
+        self._compressed_k2_buf = None
+        self._last_req_sig: Optional[Tuple[int, int]] = None  # (req_pool_idx, total_len)
+        # Env gate for A/B: SGLANG_MINICPM_COMP_K_REUSE=0 disables the reuse
+        # and falls back to the original per-layer torch.full(-inf) path.
+        self._comp_k_reuse_enabled = (
+            os.getenv("SGLANG_MINICPM_COMP_K_REUSE", "1") != "0"
+        )
+
     def update_batch_for_sparse(
         self, forward_batch: ForwardBatch, metadata: MiniCPMBackendMetadata
     ):
@@ -669,6 +690,64 @@ class MiniCPMSparseBackend(AttentionBackend):
 
         self.update_batch_for_sparse(forward_batch, metadata)
 
+        # Plan-cache-style buffer reuse for compressed_k / compressed_k2:
+        #   cross-layer — share across 8 standard attention layers in one forward
+        #   cross-chunk — skip refill when the current call is a continuation of
+        #                 the same request (extend_prefix_lens == last total_len).
+        # Refill only fires on: first call, shape change (bs flip), or request
+        # switch. See __init__ for correctness reasoning.
+        if (
+            self._comp_k_reuse_enabled
+            and forward_batch.forward_mode.is_extend_or_draft_extend_or_mixed()
+            and getattr(forward_batch, "sparse_batch_size", 0) == forward_batch.batch_size
+            and forward_batch.sparse_batch_size > 0
+        ):
+            bs = forward_batch.batch_size
+            k1_rows = bs * self.max_context_len // self.k1_kernel_stride
+            k2_rows = bs * self.max_context_len // self.k2_kernel_stride
```

stage1（flash_fwd_splitkv） | 640 | 7.0 | 136 | 4.7ms/call，top-k scoring |\n| InfLLM-v2 support（block_table/cumsum/flatten/max_pool） | 448 | 4.9 | 816 | |\n| RMSNorm | 320 | 3.5 | 2397 | |\n| SiLU-and-mul（MLP） | 312 | 3.4 | 561 | 独立 kernel |\n| NVFP4 input quantize | 239 | 2.6 | 2640 | |\n| GLA chunk_fwd | 210 | 2.3 | 768 | 24 层 Lightning Attention |\n| topk / sort | 174 | 1.9 | 2363 | |\n| RoPE | 74 | 0.8 | 425 | |\n| index_put/index_kernel | 151 | 1.7 | 8019 | |\n\nInfLLM-v2 合计 **stage1+stage2+support = 25%**，单一最大且未做 kernel 级调优的靶子。\n\n### InfLLM-v2 stage2 kernel 配置（读 flashinfer KernelTraits 模板参数）\n\n```\nKernelTraits<MaskMode=0, CTA_TILE_Q=16, NUM_MMA_Q=1, NUM_MMA_KV=1,\n             NUM_MMA_D_QK=8, NUM_MMA_D_VO=8,   // → HEAD_DIM=128\n             NUM_WARPS_Q=1, NUM_WARPS_KV=4,    // 4 warps / CTA = 128 threads\n             ...>\n```\n\nflashinfer `FA2DetermineCtaTileQ(avg_packed_qo_len, head_dim)`：\n- `avg_packed_qo_len > 64` → **128**\n- `> 16` → 64\n- `≤ 16` → **16**（命中）\n\nSALA sparse path 每 (query_token × KV_head) 拆成独立 sparse sub-batch，`seqlen_q_sparse_bs = 1`，导致 `avg_packed_qo_len = 1 ≤ 16` → 永久命中 CTA_TILE_Q=16 最小档。\n\n粗算：\n- bs=1 chunk=8192 tokens × nkv=2 = 16384 sparse sub-batches/layer/chunk\n- CTA_TILE_Q=16 packs 16 heads/CTA（`heads_per_group=16`）→ 16384 CTAs/layer/chunk\n- 每 CTA 读 top-k=112 blocks × 64 tokens × 128 dim × 2B = 1.8MB K + 1.8MB V\n- 算力利用率 ~30%（240 TFLOPS / 750 peak），memory 面高度 L2 命中\n\nkernel 接近 memory-bound 天花板但 CTA_TILE_Q=16 对纯 seqlen_q=1 的场景可能浪费 15/16 Q slot。\n\n### 失败的小优化尝试：compressed_k / compressed_k2 跨层复用（2026-04-21）\n\n**动机**：profile 里 `FillFunctor<bf16>` 225ms (2.5%)，843 calls。定位到 `minicpm_backend.get_topk_for_sparse` 的 all_sparse 分支里 `torch.full((bs×524288/16, 2, 128), -inf)` + 一个 ×(1/4) 倍的 k2 buffer **每 layer 重新分配 + fill**。130K prefill = 8 std attn 层 × 16 chunk × 2 buf = 256 次。\n\n**实现**：仿 docs/PREFILL-HOTMAP.md plan cache 套路，preallocate 到 backend 实例上，跨 layer 复用；cross-chunk 用 `(req_pool_idx, extend_prefix_lens==last_total_len)` 判据 skip fill（chunk N 的写入严格覆盖 N-1 的 slots，尾部 -inf 保留不变，正确性由 `compress_k_complete_kernel_new` 的 bounded 写入 `[0, total_chunks_in_seq)` 保证）。env gate `SGLANG_MINICPM_COMP_K_REUSE=0/1`。\n\n**同 session A/B**（plan cache 默认 on）：\n\n| 场景 | reuse ON | reuse OFF | delta |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| smax=64 (2 trials avg) | 293.76s | 292.12s | **+1.64s (ON 反而慢 0.56%)** |\n| 130K 单请求 (3 runs avg) | 11.02s | 11.12s | -0.10s |\n\n**结论：无可测收益，已 revert**。两档差距都在噪声内（smax trial 间 std ~2-3s，130K 单轮 std 0.13s）。\n\n**原因**：\n1. fill 的 200ms 大概率和前一批 kernel overlap，不在关键路径\n2. torch caching allocator 让重复同尺寸 `torch.full` 近零开销\n3. smax=64 下 per-request 小优化被互相排队延迟吞没\n\n**启示**：nsys 里 2-5% 的 FillFunctor / small elementwise 不是自动靶子。plan cache 成功是因为 per-call 20ms CPU 开销远高于 memory-bound kernel；fill 几 MB -inf 是 memory-bound，allocator 已经很好。**未来不再尝试此类对象**。\n\n## 剩余可行方向（按 ROI 排序）\n\n1. **InfLLM-v2 stage2 kernel autotune**（PLAN.md 3.6 Step 3）：13% GPU → 借 FlashPrefill config 空间（Q_TILE×K_TILE×warps×stages）重调，低风险，目标 **1.2× stage2 = ~2.6% e2e**\n2. **Stage2 CTA_TILE_Q 配置**：SALA sparse path 强制 `seqlen_q_sparse_bs=1` → FA2 选 16 最小档。研究是否可合并同 KV-head 的 Q 到更大 qo_len（会影响稀疏语义需谨慎）\n3. **α-threshold 动态稀疏率**（PLAN.md 3.6.1）：当前 fixed top-k=64+local，FlashPrefill 的 `score ≥ α × max_score` 可能让 stage2 实际 iterate 的 K 更少。**需验 accuracy**，风险中高\n4. **Python glue / small-kernel 堆**（11% of GPU）：真正 launch-bound。若要攻，方向是 piecewise cuda graph 覆盖 prefill 路径（但 plan() 是 eager 会卡住）\n"
}

221	#### 3.6.2 行动路线
222	
223	- [ ] **Step 1 (Profile)**：在 S1/S8 长 prompt (≥64K) 上 profile，量化 prefill 中 compress_k / stage1_score / stage2_FA / GLA-prefill / 其他 的时间占比。目标：找出真实瓶颈层。
224	- [ ] **Step 2 (Microbench)**：单独测 FlashPrefill `compute_mean_vector + compute_block_score` vs MiniCPM `compressed_attention` 在我们的 shape 上的时延，head_dim=128, seq∈{16K, 64K, 128K}。
225	- [ ] **Step 3 (Kernel autotune)**：用 FlashPrefill 的 configs 空间重调 `minicpm_sparse_kernels.py` 里 stage2 FA 的 Q_TILE/K_TILE/warps/stages，低风险尝试 ~1.2× 收益。
226	- [ ] **Step 4 (α-threshold 可选)**：如果 Step 1 显示 stage2 FA 是瓶颈，且 stage1 的 block 选择有大量 near-zero score，考虑改成 α-threshold + min_budget 兜底。需要 accuracy 验证（local mcq + 人话 check）。
227	- [ ] **Step 5 (block-sparse FA 替换)**：如果 microbench 显示 FlashPrefill 的 `_flash_forward` kernel 更快，用它替换 stage2（需适配 SGLang paged KV + varlen 接口）。
228	
229	**备注**：FlashPrefill 原生 targets vLLM + HF attention，移植到 SGLang 要接 `minicpm_backend` 的 SparseMetadata + paged cache。Step 1-3 是低风险离线验证，Step 4-5 需要完整 e2e bench + accuracy。
